TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成大语言模型（LLM）的死亡率预测接口，通过将自然语言输入转化为结构化配置，在保持统计可靠性的前提下提升了非专业用户的可及性。采用三阶段方法：基于CoMoMo包建立基线管道、滚动窗口评估生成多步预测、以及本地LLM原型接口处理自然语言请求。实验证明，LLM可在不牺牲可复现性、透明度和精算有效性的前提下增强高 stakes 分析工作流的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 死亡率预测在精算和政策制定中至关重要，但其技术复杂性使得非专业用户难以使用。当前缺乏一个既保持统计效力又具备良好可用性的工具，因此作者希望构建一个LLM集成的友好接口来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 三阶段方法：1) 使用CoMoMo包实现基线死亡率预测管道；2) 扩展管道，采用滚动起始验证和均方误差（MSE）生成多步预测；3) 构建原型接口，利用本地LLM将用户的自然语言请求转化为结构化的预测配置参数，LLM作为受约束的编排层。

Result: 系统证明LLM能够有效增强死亡率预测工具的可访问性，同时不损害可复现性、透明度或精算有效性，适用于高风险分析工作流。

Conclusion: LLM可以作为受约束的编排层，在保持死亡率预测统计严谨性的同时显著降低使用门槛，为精算和决策领域提供了一种可行的增强方案。

Abstract: Mortality forecasting plays an important role in actuarial and policy decision-making, but its implementation remains technically complex and inaccessible to non-expert users. This project proposes a reliable large language model (LLM)-integrated interface that improves usability while maintaining statistical power. The LLM is designed as a constrained orchestration layer that translates natural-language inputs into structured configurations for a deterministic forecasting pipeline. A three-phase methodology is employed to ensure accuracy, usability, and transparency. First, a baseline pipeline is implemented using the CoMoMo package, reproducing established mortality forecasting results. Second, the pipeline is extended to generate multi-step forecasts using rolling-origin evaluation and mean squared error (MSE). Third, a prototype interface uses a local LLM to handle users' forecasting requests in plain language. The system demonstrates that LLMs can enhance accessibility without compromising reproducibility, transparency, or actuarial validity in high-stakes analytical workflows.

</details>


### [150] [Tracing the Oracle: Improving Diffusion Timestep Scheduling for 3D CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06236)
*Yujia Wu,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TrO框架，通过在少量样本上密集采样数值积分轨迹作为参考"Oracle"，利用动态规划全局最小化多步近似与Oracle之间的累积误差，优化扩散模型在3D CT重建中的时间步调度，显著提升计算效率和重建质量，尤其在≤10步的严格预算下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练扩散模型在解决3D CT严重病态逆问题时推理计算开销大，且均匀时间步调度无法捕捉反向条件扩散SDE的非均匀演化，导致显著的截断误差。

Method: 提出TrO（Tracing the Oracle）即插即用框架。在少量样本上密集采样数值积分轨迹作为参考Oracle，利用动态规划全局最小化多步近似与Oracle之间的累积误差，从而将有限采样步骤精确分配到对截断误差高度敏感的关键演化阶段。

Result: 在AAPM数据集上的多类3D CT重建任务中，TrO优化的时间步调度相比现有启发式调度，显著提升了重建保真度和计算效率，尤其在≤10步的严格采样预算下表现突出。

Conclusion: TrO是一种有效的即插即用时间步优化框架，能够在不改变模型结构的前提下，通过数据驱动的方式显著提升扩散模型在3D CT重建中的性能与效率。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models demonstrate impressive potential in solving highly ill-posed 3D computed tomography (CT) inverse problems, while the inference process suffers from significant computational overhead. Furthermore, existing uniform timestep schedules fail to capture the non-uniform evolution of the reverse conditional diffusion stochastic differential equation, thereby introducing substantial truncation errors. To overcome this limitation, we propose Tracing the Oracle (TrO), a plug-and-play framework for improved timestep scheduling. Specifically, we treat densely sampled numerical integration trajectories on a few samples as the reference oracle. The optimized schedule is extracted by leveraging dynamic programming to globally minimize the cumulative error between the few-step approximation and the oracle. This mechanism precisely allocates the limited sampling steps to critical evolution stages that are highly susceptible to truncation errors. Our extensive experiments on the AAPM dataset across multiple 3D CT reconstruction tasks demonstrate that, when combined with the state-of-the-art 3D CT reconstruction method DDS, our optimized timesteps significantly improve reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency compared to existing heuristic schedules, especially under a strict budget of no more than 10 sampling steps.

</details>


### [151] [Generative Criticality in Large Language Model Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06238)
*Huajian Ruan,Jinyang Li,Xingyu Guo,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统计场框架，将LLM生成的token嵌入视为一维链上的连续自旋变量，发现softmax温度T附近存在类似连续相变的尖锐磁化率峰、序参量突变、语义方向坍缩及本征维度极小值，且该现象在多种模型规模（Qwen3: 0.6B–32B）和提示类别上具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解和量化LLM生成文本的集体统计结构，探索解码策略与临界现象之间的潜在联系，建立一套定量分析工具。

Method: 将token嵌入视为一维伊辛链上的连续自旋变量，定义连接两点关联函数给出磁化率，定义系综平均嵌入场为序参量；通过改变softmax温度T观测上述量的行为，并利用TwoNN方法估计本征维度进行独立验证。

Result: 在特征温度Tc附近观察到：(1) 磁化率呈现尖锐峰值并具有幂律标度行为；(2) 序参量发生快速变化；(3) Tc以下嵌入坍缩到单一语义方向；(4) TwoNN本征维度在Tc附近达到最小值。结果在Qwen3系列（0.6B–32B）及多种提示类别上具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM生成过程表现出与连续相变高度相似的现象学特征，但其非平衡自回归性质仍需进一步研究；该框架为探测LLM输出的集体统计结构提供了定量工具，并建立了解码策略与临界现象之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We propose a statistical-field framework for text generated by large language models (LLMs), treating token embeddings as continuous spin variables on a one-dimensional chain. Defining a susceptibility from the connected two-point correlator and an order parameter from the ensemble-averaged embedding field, we vary the \texttt{softmax} temperature $T$ and observe a sharp susceptibility peak near a characteristic $T_c$ with power-law-like scaling, a concurrent rapid change in the order parameter, and a collapse onto a single semantic direction below $T_c$. The intrinsic dimension estimated by the two nearest neighbor (TwoNN) method independently corroborates these findings, reaching a minimum near $T_c$. Results are robust across model scales (Qwen3: 0.6B--32B) and prompt categories. While the phenomenology closely resembles a continuous phase transition, the non-equilibrium nature of autoregressive generation warrants further investigation. Our framework provides quantitative tools for probing the collective statistical structure of LLM outputs and suggests connections between decoding strategies and critical phenomena.

</details>


### [152] [Your GFlowNet Secretly Learns an Optimal Transport Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06272)
*Ian Maksimov,Nikita Morozov,Denis Belomestny,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了非无环GFlowNets与最优传输（OT）之间的理论联系，证明最小流GFlowNet的目标函数可简化为具有图诱导最短路径代价的Kantorovich OT问题，且最优解下GFlowNet策略编码了源分布到目标分布的最优传输计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非无环GFlowNets与最优传输之间的理论桥梁，将GFlowNet学习框架扩展到最优传输问题。

Method: 通过固定最小流GFlowNet中的初始流分布，将其目标函数简化为Kantorovich OT问题，利用图诱导最短路径代价作为传输成本。

Result: GFlowNet策略在最优解下编码了最优传输计划；采样轨迹可恢复对应最优耦合；实验与精确OT求解器结果一致。

Conclusion: GFlowNet学习框架可有效应用于大规模图上的最优传输问题，通过边流和神经参数化学习高质量传输计划。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) are a framework for sampling structured objects via stochastic trajectories in a directed graph. In this work, we establish a theoretical connection between non-acyclic GFlowNets and optimal transport (OT). We show that fixing the initial flow distribution in a minimum-flow GFlowNet reduces its objective to a Kantorovich OT problem with graph-induced shortest path costs. At the optimum, the learned GFlowNet policy therefore encodes an optimal transport plan from the source distribution to the target distribution: we show that sampling trajectories from the minimum-flow GFlowNet recovers the corresponding optimal coupling. Our formulation enables applying the GFlowNet learning framework to OT problems on large graphs via edge flows and neural parameterization. Experiments confirm agreement with exact OT solvers and demonstrate that GFlowNets can learn high-quality transport plans.

</details>


### [153] [PAC-Bayesian Adversarially Robust Generalization for Message Passing Graph Neural Networks: A Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06293)
*Ziling Liang,Xinping Yi,Qingsong Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图神经网络（GNN）在对抗攻击下的鲁棒泛化问题，将基于敏感性的PAC-Bayesian框架从深度神经网络扩展到消息传递GNN（MPGNN），通过利用输出Jacobian矩阵的低秩性质（秩至多为类别数K）构建各向异性高斯后验，推导出更紧的鲁棒泛化上界，将主导维度因子从隐藏宽度降低为类别数K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对GNN的鲁棒泛化分析通常采用各向同性高斯后验并在全参数空间控制权重扰动，难以捕捉不同参数块的异质性敏感性，且依赖隐藏宽度相关的复杂度项，导致泛化界不够紧致。

Method: 1) 量化不同参数块对网络输出的敏感性（通过输出Jacobian矩阵）；2) 利用K类图分类中Jacobian矩阵秩至多为K的性质，构造Jacobian对齐的敏感性矩阵；3) 使用优化协方差的各向异性高斯后验来紧凑地约束KL散度；4) 将谱范数依赖从隐藏宽度降低为类别数K。

Result: 得到了比现有工作更紧的MPGNN鲁棒泛化保证，主导维度因子从隐藏宽度项降低为类别数K，能够更好地指导具有对抗鲁棒性的模型设计。

Conclusion: 基于敏感性的PAC-Bayesian框架可有效应用于MPGNN的鲁棒泛化分析，通过利用Jacobian矩阵的低秩特性构造各向异性后验，可获得更紧的鲁棒泛化界。

Abstract: Whilst the vulnerability of graph neural networks (GNNs) to adversarial attacks poses a critical threat to graph representation learning, the understanding of the robust generalization behavior remains a fundamental challenge in the adversarial setting. Recently, PAC-Bayesian margin-based generalization analysis substantially advances this line of research by providing a flexible and data-dependent analytical framework. However, existing robust analyses often rely on isotropic Gaussian posteriors and control weight perturbations in the full parameter space, which limits the ability to capture heterogeneous parameter sensitivity yet hinges on hidden-width-dependent complexity terms, resulting in not-tight-enough generalization bounds. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed sensitivity-aware PAC-Bayesian framework from deep neural networks to message passing GNNs (MPGNNs) and derive a tighter robust generalization bound in the adversarial setting. Specifically, we first quantify how sensitive the perturbations across different parameter blocks are to the network outputs by deriving the output Jacobians with respect to the weight parameters. Exploiting the fact that these Jacobian matrices have rank at most $K$ in $K$-class graph classification, we then construct Jacobian-aligned sensitivity matrices and use anisotropic Gaussian posteriors with optimized covariances to upper bound the KL divergence in a tight way. Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [154] [Reactive Flux Matching: Mechanism Discovery and Adaptive Sampling of Rare Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06295)
*Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes,Nicholas M. Boffi,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flux Matching框架，从反应轨迹数据中学习电流速度u(z)和标量势h(z)，分别用于识别主导反应路径和作为数据驱动反应坐标，无需动力学或稳态分布先验知识，在分子系统上验证了其生成电流速度轨迹和计算速率常数的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径采样方法虽能生成连接亚稳态的反应轨迹集合，但从中提取机制性见解仍然困难，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出Flux Matching框架，通过对反应路径系综最小化二次泛函，学习电流速度u(z)（其流线追踪主导反应路径）和标量势h(z)（通过反应电流的加权Helmholtz-Hodge分解获得），类似生成模型中的flow matching损失。

Result: 在分子系统上验证了该方法能生成电流速度轨迹并准确计算速率常数。

Conclusion: Flux Matching无需动力学或稳态分布先验知识，且在投影到非马尔可夫集体变量时仍保持良好定义，其水平集可为增强采样方法提供自适应界面，优于基于committor的方法。

Abstract: Path sampling methods generate ensembles of reactive trajectories connecting metastable states, but extracting mechanistic insight from these data remains nontrivial. We introduce Flux Matching, a framework that learns two complementary objects directly from reactive trajectory data: a current velocity $u(z)$, whose streamlines trace the dominant reaction pathways, and a scalar potential $h(z)$, obtained from a weighted Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition of the reactive current, that serves as a data-driven reaction coordinate. Both minimize quadratic functionals over the reactive path ensemble, analogous to the flow matching loss in generative modeling, and require no knowledge of the underlying dynamics or stationary distribution. Unlike committor-based methods, $u$ and $h$ remain well-defined under projection onto non-Markovian collective variables, and their level sets in turn provide adaptive interfaces for improved sampling with enhanced sampling methods. Flux Matching is validated through the generation of current velocity trajectories and rate constant calculations on molecular systems.

</details>


### [155] [Tangram: Unlocking Non-Uniform KV Cache for Efficient Multi-turn LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06302)
*Hyungmin Kim,Minsoo Kim,Hongseok Kim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tangram提出了一种面向非均匀KV缓存的高效LLM服务系统，通过确定性预算分配、头组页面聚类和提前负载均衡三大技术，在完全保持模型精度的前提下，吞吐量提升高达2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM服务中KV缓存线性增长给GPU内存和带宽带来巨大压力。非均匀KV压缩虽能保留更多信息，但其异构性导致内存碎片化、调度复杂度和内核利用率下降等系统性问题，现有服务系统效率低下。

Method: (1) 确定性预算分配：基于每个注意力头的固有模式分配静态内存占用，消除动态调度开销和预填充停顿；(2) 头组页面聚类：将相似保留需求的注意力头聚类，用独立向量化页表管理，最大化物理内存回收；(3) 提前负载均衡：利用静态预算配置确保GPU均匀利用，无运行时开销。

Result: 相比现有基线方法，吞吐量提升高达2.6倍，且完全保持模型精度。

Conclusion: Tangram通过系统性设计有效解决了非均匀KV缓存在实际部署中的多种效率瓶颈，实现了高性能且无损精度的LLM服务。

Abstract: Multi-turn Large Language Model (LLM) serving is critical for consistent user experiences, yet the linear growth of the Key-Value (KV) cache imposes significant pressure on GPU memory and bandwidth. Non-uniform KV compression effectively preserves more information by considering the individual importance of each KV cache. However, such KV cache heterogeneity introduces various systemic challenges - including memory fragmentation, scheduling complexities, and diminished kernel utilization - which collectively lead to significant inefficiencies in existing LLM serving systems.
  To overcome these challenges, we present Tangram, a novel serving system designed to make Non-uniform KV caches practical. Tangram addresses systemic inefficiencies through three core techniques: (1) Deterministic Budget Allocation assigns a static memory footprint to each head based on its intrinsic pattern, entirely eliminating dynamic scheduling overhead and prefill stalls; (2) Head Group Page clusters attention heads with similar retention demands and manages them with independent, vectorized page tables, thereby maximizing physical memory reclamation; and (3) Ahead-of-Time (AOT) Load Balancing leverages static budget profiles to ensure uniform GPU utilization without runtime overhead. Experimental results show that Tangram improves throughput by up to 2.6x compared to existing baselines, while fully preserving model accuracy. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/aiha-lab/TANGRAM.

</details>


### [156] [Plug-and-Play Guidance for Discrete Diffusion Models via Gradient-Informed Logit Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06303)
*Hongkun Dou,Zike Chen,Fengji Li,Hongjue Li,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GILC，一种即插即用的框架，通过将预训练去噪网络作为变分代理来高效估计引导信号，无需重新训练。针对高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定性，引入无雅可比机制直接校正干净预测logits，支持可微和不可微奖励函数。在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上达到SOTA，常优于微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型的可控生成常面临高计算开销或需要重新训练的问题，现有方法在高效引导离散生成方面存在不足。

Method: 提出GILC框架，利用预训练去噪网络作为变分代理估计引导信号；引入无雅可比（Jacobian-free）机制直接校正干净预测logits，避免高维离散空间中的梯度不稳定性；支持可微和不可微奖励函数。

Result: 在DNA、蛋白质序列和分子生成任务上达到最优性能，无需额外训练，且常优于需要微调的方法。

Conclusion: GILC作为一种即插即用的高效引导方法，在离散扩散模型可控生成中无需重新训练即可取得SOTA效果，验证了其通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Controllable generation with discrete diffusion models is often hindered by high computational overhead or the need for retraining. In this paper, we present \underline{\textbf{G}}radient-\underline{\textbf{I}}nformed \underline{\textbf{L}}ogit \underline{\textbf{C}}orrection (\textbf{GILC}), a plug-and-play framework that efficiently estimates guidance signals by repurposing the pretrained denoising network as a variational proxy. To circumvent the gradient instability inherent in high-dimensional discrete spaces, we introduce a Jacobian-free mechanism that directly corrects the clean prediction logits, facilitating stable and effective guidance. Our method accommodates both differentiable and non-differentiable reward functions. Extensive experiments across DNA, protein sequence, and molecular generation tasks demonstrate that GILC achieves state-of-the-art performance without additional training, frequently outperforming fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [157] [Learning What to Forget: Improving LLM Unlearning via Learned Token-Level Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06320)
*Gizem Yüce,Giorgos Nikolaou,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于保留目标冲突度量的无监督令牌级遗忘权重学习方法ATWU，通过联合优化模型参数和令牌权重实现更精确的机器遗忘，在TOFU和RWKU基准上达到最优的遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型中，遗忘样本的不同令牌对遗忘目标的重要性不同。现有方法要么忽略这种异质性，要么依赖辅助模型、启发式规则或外部标注来估计每个令牌的遗忘相关性，缺乏高效且无监督的解决方案。

Method: 提出ATWU（交替令牌加权遗忘）框架：1) 将遗忘问题形式化为模型参数和令牌权重的联合优化问题，通过保留目标冲突度量化令牌的遗忘特异性；2) 使用轻量级线性评分器基于隐藏状态学习令牌权重，无需外部令牌级监督；3) 在自然分离条件下证明该目标能恢复Oracle遗忘令牌支持集。

Result: 在TOFU和RWKU基准上，ATWU在遗忘-保留权衡上达到最优性能，优于样本级方法、基于概率的令牌加权启发式方法和辅助模型方法。学习到的分数与真实遗忘特异性片段的对齐程度显著更好。

Conclusion: 保留冲突是识别语言模型应遗忘内容的有效准则，能够以最小计算开销直接从模型表示中无监督地学习令牌级遗忘特异性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted knowledge from a trained model while preserving its general capabilities. For autoregressive language models, not all tokens in a forget sample are equally relevant to forgetting. Existing approaches either ignore this heterogeneity or rely on auxiliary models, heuristics, or external annotations to estimate each token's relevance for forgetting. We instead characterize it through the interaction with the retain objective: a token is forget-specific to the extent that minimizing the forget loss on that token does not conflict with retain optimality. We formalize this perspective as a joint optimization problem over the model parameters and the token weights and show that, under a natural separation condition, the resulting objective recovers the oracle forget-specific token support. Motivated by this formulation, we introduce Alternating Token-Weighted Unlearning (ATWU), a lightweight framework that jointly learns token forget-specificity and model parameters during unlearning using a simple linear scorer over the hidden states, without external token level supervision. Across TOFU and RWKU, ATWU achieves state of the art forget-retain trade-offs, outperforming sample-level methods, probability-based token weighting heuristics, and auxiliary-model-based approaches. Moreover, the learned scores align substantially better with ground truth forget-specific spans, indicating that ATWU identifies semantically meaningful token level forgetting signals. Overall, our results suggest that retain conflict provides an effective criterion for identifying what language models should forget, enabling unsupervised learning of token level forget-specificity directly from model representations with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [158] [PAMF: Prior-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Incomplete Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06328)
*Ziwen Kan,Wugeng Zheng,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAMF是一种面向多模态医疗时间序列的框架，通过先验感知的流匹配和权重共享，显式处理模态内缺失和模态级缺失两种模式，并将插补与下游预测任务耦合，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多模态时间序列缺失时存在两个关键问题：(1) 通常通过掩码或缺失嵌入隐式表示未观测数据，未学习实例特定的缺失信息；(2) 大多数方法只针对单一缺失模式设计，且现有插补方法对不同结构性先验的缺失模式处理方式相同，插补过程与下游任务相互隔离，无法引导插补生成更具信息量的表示。

Method: 提出PAMF框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 使用类型特定的先验初始化流匹配的源状态，以区分模态内缺失和模态级缺失两种类型；(2) 通过架构匹配的编码器和权重共享将插补与分类任务连接，将任务相关表示传递到插补过程中。

Result: 在多个多模态医疗时间序列基准数据集上，PAMF在不同数据集和缺失设置下均取得了最优的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 显式处理不同缺失模式的结构性差异，并通过权重共享耦合插补与下游任务，能有效提升多模态时间序列在缺失场景下的下游任务表现。

Abstract: In healthcare, multimodal time series tasks often operate on incomplete observations in practice, for example when ECG segments are lost because electrodes detach or an entire respiratory channel is unavailable during overnight monitoring. Such missingness typically appears in two structurally distinct patterns: within-modality missing, where values are absent within an otherwise observed modality, and modality-level missing, where an entire modality is unavailable. Existing methods typically represent unobserved data implicitly through masks or missing embeddings, without learning instance-specific missing information, and most are designed for only one missingness pattern. A natural approach is to explicitly estimate the missing data; however, existing imputation methods treat missingness uniformly despite their different structural priors, and the imputation process is often isolated from downstream tasks, preventing downstream tasks from guiding imputation toward more informative representations. To address these limitations, we present PAMF, a multimodal time-series framework that explicitly handles different missingness patterns while coupling imputation with downstream prediction through prior-aware flow matching and weight sharing. Specifically, the method initializes the flow-matching source state with type-specific priors to distinguish two missing types. It further connects imputation and classification through architecturally matched encoders with weight sharing, transferring task-relevant representations into the imputation process. Experiments on multiple multimodal healthcare time-series benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves the strongest overall downstream performance across diverse datasets and missing settings compared with existing baselines.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维数据局部平均曲率计算中的O(m^4)复杂度瓶颈，提出两项贡献：一是通过代数恒等式将复杂度降至O(m^2)，二是利用截断SVD和Haar测度下的零空间近似将总复杂度降至O(k²m + kmp²)，在真实数据集上实现50-300倍加速且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据局部平均曲率计算是几何感知机器学习算法（如MCBP）的关键，但原始O(m^4)的朴素实现无法处理特征数超过几十个的数据集，亟需可扩展的高效计算方法。

Method: 1) 利用协方差矩阵特征向量正交性和迹算子的循环性，推导精确代数恒等式消除显式H矩阵构造，将每点复杂度从O(m^4)降至O(m^2)；2) 用k×m中心化数据矩阵的截断SVD（O(k²m)）替代全特征分解（O(m³)），并基于Haar测度下零空间外积的期望值导出解析近似，最终复杂度为O(k²m + kmp²)，其中p=k-1。

Result: 在真实数据集上相比原始实现获得50-300倍加速比，快速估计器替代原始版本时精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提出的可扩展数据驱动局部曲率估计方法，使曲率成为从经典机器学习到现代深度学习流水线中切实可用的几何特征。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [160] [Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Effective Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06333)
*Seyed Arshan Dalili,Mehrdad Mahdavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出稀疏自编码器（SAE）假设每个潜在特征为一维向量，与模型特征的多维结构不匹配，导致特征分裂问题。作者从几何和优化角度证明了这种分裂（原子数量随维度指数增长）。为此提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA），用可学习的子空间替代单向量解码器，结合块稀疏门控和核范数正则化。理论证明当块大小足够时，单个组即可表示完整特征切片且是全局最优解，样本复杂度从指数降为多项式。在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上，SASA减少特征分裂和吸收，提升单语义性和可解释性，用约一半token预算达到或超过标准SAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE假设每个潜在特征是一维的，这与大语言模型内部特征实际上多维结构不匹配，导致特征分裂（feature splitting）现象：一个完整特征被碎片化到多个近共线的潜在变量中，造成虚假多义性并模糊内在几何结构。

Method: 提出子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA）：(1) 用可学习的子空间替换单向量解码器；(2) 通过Top-s组门控实现块稀疏性；(3) 用核范数正则化自适应调整每个组的有效秩。理论分析了单方向解码器导致特征分裂的几何机制（维度诅咒）和优化机制（L1正则化SAE目标的下降方向驱动指数分裂）。

Result: 在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上，SASA相比标准SAE减少特征分裂和吸收，提升单语义性和可解释性，用约一半token预算达到或超过标准SAE性能。

Conclusion: 标准SAE的特征一维假设是根本性缺陷，SASA通过子空间解码器从理论和实践上解决了特征分裂问题，实现了多项式而非指数级的样本复杂度，为可解释性分析提供了更忠实于模型内在几何结构的工具。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used for mechanistic interpretability in large language models, yet their formulation assigns each latent feature a single decoder direction, implicitly assuming features to be one-dimensional. We show that this assumption mismatches with the multi-dimensional structure of model features, provably inducing feature splitting through two distinct mechanisms. Geometrically, reconstructing a feature of intrinsic dimension $d_i \ge 2$ to error $\varepsilon$ with single-direction decoders forces a number of atoms that is exponential in $d_i$. From an end-to-end optimization perspective, this splitting is not merely possible but actively preferred. We prove that there exists a continuous path from the true $d_i$-dimensional basis to a strictly lower risk of the $\ell_1$-regularized SAE objective, whose descent directions drive any trained dictionary into that exponential regime. A single coherent feature is therefore fragmented across many near-collinear latents, producing spurious multiplicity and obscuring the intrinsic geometry. Motivated by this, we introduce Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders (SASA), which replace single-vector decoders with learned decoder subspaces, enforce block sparsity via Top-$s$ group gating, and adapt each group's effective rank with a nuclear-norm regularizer. We then show that once the block size satisfies $r \ge d_i$, a single group not only can represent the entire feature slice but is the global minimizer of the SASA objective. This consolidation yields a sample complexity polynomial in $d_i$ rather than exponential -- a decisive advantage given that every training activation costs an LLM forward pass. Empirically, on GPT-2 and Mistral-7B, SASA reduces feature splitting and absorption, improves monosemanticity and interpretability, and matches or exceeds standard SAEs while training on roughly half the token budget.

</details>


### [161] [Quantifying the Privacy of Counterfactuals by Leveraging Membership Inference Attacks Against Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06334)
*Maryam Babaei,Yingke Wang,Hadrien Lautraite,Heber H. Arcolezi,Ulrich Aivodji,Sebastien Gambs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了反事实解释（counterfactuals）可能被对手利用进行隐私攻击，特别是通过借鉴针对合成数据的成员推理攻击方法，在无需访问原始模型的情况下，仅凭反事实数据即可成功实施成员推理攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释常用于高风险决策领域，但其发布可能带来隐私泄露风险。作者试图探究对手能否利用反事实数据对模型或其训练数据实施隐私攻击。

Method: 借鉴针对合成数据的成员推理攻击方法，将其应用于各类反事实数据；同时设计了一种无需访问原始模型、仅凭反事实数据即可实施的成员推理攻击方案。

Result: 针对合成数据的成员推理攻击成功适用于多种类型的反事实数据；仅凭反事实数据（无模型访问权限）即可成功实施成员推理攻击。

Conclusion: 模型开发者在发布反事实解释时应更加谨慎，因为反事实数据可能导致训练数据的隐私泄露。

Abstract: Counterfactuals are typically used in high-stakes decision areas to explain a machine learning model by showing how changes to the user profiles result in the desired outcome. However, explaining the model's decisions through counterfactuals can also be exploited by an adversary to conduct privacy attacks against the model or its training data. Drawing on the analogy that counterfactuals provide realistic substitutes for real training data, similar to synthetic data, we demonstrate in this paper how it is possible to successfully perform privacy attacks on counterfactuals by drawing on the attacks developed against synthetic data. More precisely, we investigate the effectiveness of the membership inference attacks designed for synthetic data on various types of counterfactuals. Additionally, while existing membership inference attacks against counterfactuals usually require to be able to query the model, we show how it is possible to perform successful membership inference attacks using only a set of counterfactuals, with no access to the model from which they are generated. Our results demonstrate that model developers should be more cautious when releasing counterfactuals to various users, as it can lead to a privacy breach.

</details>


### [162] [Bridging Domain Expertise and Generalization for Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06335)
*Shuxuan Li,Zhilin Zhao,Quyu Kong,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FRAP方法，通过融合基础模型与外部基础模型的互补优势，在分布偏移下更可靠地估计模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性能估计方法仅依赖单一模型的输出，在分布偏移下模型偏差被放大，导致与真实性能的相关性减弱。

Method: FRAP利用外部基础模型与基础模型互补，通过温度缩放校准对齐两者的预测分布，再基于置信度加权融合为参考分布，以基础模型与该参考分布的一致程度估计性能。

Result: 在多种数据集和架构下，FRAP相比代表性方法在分布偏移下取得了一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 融合外部基础模型的鲁棒性与基础模型的领域专长，能构建更可靠的性能估计指标。

Abstract: Performance estimation under distribution shift aims to predict how a model behaves on an unlabeled test set whose distribution differs from the training data, a scenario that requires reliable indicators that can faithfully reflect model behavior without ground-truth labels. Existing approaches rely solely on the outputs of the given model whose biases are amplified once the distribution shifts, weakening the correlation with the true performance. Motivated by this limitation, we propose Fused Reference Alignment Prediction (FRAP), which leverages the complementary strengths of an external foundation model and the base model to construct a more reliable surrogate of the ground-truth labels. FRAP aligns the prediction distribution of the foundation model with that of the base model by applying temperature-scaled calibration that minimizes their divergence. The aligned predictions are fused through confidence-based weighting into a refined reference distribution that integrates robustness from the foundation model and domain-specific expertise from the base model, and performance estimation is obtained by measuring how closely the base model predictions agree with this reference. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and architectures show that FRAP provides consistent and substantial improvements over representative performance-estimation methods under distribution shift.

</details>


### [163] [Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06344)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出等变神经信念传播（ENBP），一种因子图框架，其消息为等变高斯混合模型，支持SE(3)对称性下的秩2精度矩阵合成。在GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs上实现98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差和亚秒级延迟，比扩散基线快100倍以上；在多体机器人推理中，在15+智能体场景下收敛，具有近零碰撞率和机器精度等变误差（~10⁻⁷ vs. 10⁻¹）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变网络仅能产生标量和向量，无法输出各向异性不确定性所需的秩2精度张量；同时单分量消息会崩溃多模态能量景观为物理无意义的平均值。

Method: 构建因子图框架，消息为等变高斯混合模型；通过等变外积合成秩2精度矩阵，经可微谱分解处理，利用贪心KL散度基混合约简保证与SE(3)可交换。

Result: GEOM-QM9和GEOM-Drugs：98.9%构象覆盖率、0.090Å误差、亚秒级延迟，比扩散基线快100倍以上；多体机器人推理：15+智能体下收敛，近零碰撞率，等变误差~10⁻⁷（基线为10⁻¹）。

Conclusion: ENBP通过等变高斯混合消息和秩2精度矩阵合成，在分子构象生成和多体机器人推理任务中同时实现了高精度、高效率和高可靠性。

Abstract: Probabilistic inference over spatially embedded variables requires beliefs that respect $SE(3)$ symmetry, yet existing equivariant networks produce only scalars and vectors -- not the rank-2 precision tensors needed for anisotropic uncertainty, and single-component messages collapse multi-modal energy landscapes to physically meaningless averages. We introduce Equivariant Neural Belief Propagation (ENBP), a factor-graph framework whose messages are equivariant Gaussian mixture models with sufficient statistics that transform exactly under $SE(3)$. Rank-2 precision matrices are synthesised via equivariant outer products, ingested through differentiable spectral decomposition, and kept tractable by a greedy KL-based mixture reduction that provably commutes with $SE(3)$. On GEOM-QM9 and GEOM-Drugs, ENBP achieves 98.9% conformational coverage at 0.090 $\mathring{A}$ error with sub-second latency -- over $100\times$ faster than diffusion baselines at higher accuracy. On multi-body robotic inference, vanilla loopy BP diverges at 15+ agents while ENBP converges with near-zero collision rates and machine-precision equivariance error (${\sim}10^{-7}$ vs.\ $10^{-1}$ for augmented baselines).

</details>


### [164] [Performance Evaluation of GraphCast for Medium-Range Weather Forecasting over Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06348)
*Wolfgang R. Rowell,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GraphCast与ECMWF IFS HRES在巴西四个气候次区域的预报表现，发现GraphCast在澳洲冬季中程（2-7天）对快速传播的斜压系统表现不佳，但在延伸期因平滑混沌小尺度变率而占优；在澳洲夏季湿季则能准确捕获大尺度水汽输送，同时抑制高频对流变率。研究为巴西建立了MLWP基准，并为未来AI模型的“热带化”优化指明了物理边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据驱动的机器学习天气预报模型（MLWP）在全局尺度上展现出卓越技能，但在全球南方（如巴西）等复杂、强对流环境下的区域评估极为匮乏，其实际有效性尚未得到验证。

Method: 采用可扩展的云端原生流水线和WeatherBench-X框架，以运行态IFS分析为真值，选取四个巴西气候次区域、四个季节性窗口，评估GraphCast与确定性ECMWF IFS HRES在三个对流层变量（$T_{850}$、$Q_{850}$、$Z_{500}$）上的表现，并计算统计指标。

Result: GraphCast的表现呈现态依赖特征：澳洲冬季中程（2-7天）在$Z_{500}$上对快速斜压系统解析不足；延伸期因平滑效应占优。澳洲夏季湿季能准确捕获大尺度水汽输送，但内在抑制高频对流变率，从而改善确定性NWP温度预报。

Conclusion: 该研究为巴西建立了MLWP评估基线，明确了具体物理约束边界，为未来针对区域韧性的AI模型“热带化”优化提供指导。

Abstract: The paradigm of global weather forecasting is rapidly shifting with the emergence of Machine Learning Weather Prediction models (MLWP). While these data-driven architectures demonstrate remarkable global skill, regional benchmarks in the Global South remain scarce, leaving their efficacy in complex, highly convective environments largely unverified. This study evaluates the performance of GraphCast operational against the deterministic ECMWF IFS HRES as baseline across four distinct Brazilian climatic sub-regions. Utilizing a scalable, cloud-native pipeline and the WeatherBench-X framework for benchmarking weather models, we assess selected tropospheric variables ($T_{850}$, $Q_{850}$, $Z_{500}$) over four selected seasonal windows, employing the operational IFS analysis as the ground truth to calculate the statistical metrics for both models. Results reveal a regime-dependent skill profile. During the austral winter, GraphCast underperforms in the medium range (lead days 2-7) for $Z_{500}$ when resolving fast-propagating baroclinic systems over southern Brazil, but regains an advantage in the extended range, where its inherent smoothing of chaotic small-scale variability becomes beneficial under deterministic skill metrics. Conversely, during the austral summer wet season, GraphCast accurately captures large-scale moisture transport while intrinsically dampening the high-frequency convective variability that degrades deterministic NWP temperature forecasts. These findings establish a baseline for Brazil and define the specific physical boundaries that will guide future ``tropicalization'' efforts, aiming to optimize these foundational AI models for regional resilience.

</details>


### [165] [Maximising the Set-Piece Return: Optimising Football Corner Tactics with Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06353)
*Sean Groom,Michael Groom,Francisco Belo,Axl Rice,Liam Anderson,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图强化学习的任意球战术优化方法，通过调整进攻球员位置和速度最大化首次触球射门概率，在3000+英超角球数据上显著优于传统优化方法，实现了从历史模仿到奖励驱动战术发现的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球战术分析方法局限于历史动作表征或分析师指定的反事实场景，无法发现新的可泛化的球员配置与策略。

Method: 将角球战术优化建模为决策问题，提出基于图结构数据的强化学习架构，中央策略调整进攻球员位置和速度以最大化首次触球射门概率，输出可处理任意初始球员位置的通用策略。

Result: 在3000+英超角球数据集上，该方法在匹配推理预算下显著优于基线优化技术。

Conclusion: 图强化学习能够将定位球分析从历史评估与模仿转向奖励驱动的战术发现。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly employed for the evaluation of football tactics. However, existing approaches focus on characterising historical actions or analyst-specified counterfactual scenarios. In this work, we seek to go beyond the imitation of historically observed patterns towards discovering new generalisable player configurations and strategies. To tackle this, we focus on optimising corner kick routines, and formulate a decision-making problem in which a central policy makes adjustments to attacking player positions and velocities to maximise first contact shot probability. Unlike classic optimisation that solves for isolated setups, we contribute a reinforcement learning architecture operating on graph-structured data that yields a general policy for adjusting arbitrary starting player positions. Evaluated on over 3,000 Premier League corners, our approach strongly outperforms baseline optimisation techniques under matched inference budgets. Our results suggest that graph reinforcement learning can shift set-piece analysis from historical evaluation and imitation towards reward-driven tactical discovery.

</details>


### [166] [End-to-End Subgraph Detection with GraphDETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06364)
*Dexiong Chen,Till Hendrik Schulz,Karsten Borgwardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDETR是一个基于深度学习的子图检测框架，将子图检测建模为集合预测问题，借鉴了DETR目标检测的思想，通过图神经网络编码目标图，使用可学习的查询向量和Transformer解码器一次性预测所有模式出现的位置，支持精确匹配和近似匹配，在包含多达1000个节点的目标图上检测高达50个节点的模式，并在ChEMBL分子功能团检测任务中达到AP100=91.2的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测是多个科学领域的基础问题，但与子图同构问题密切相关，后者是NP完全的，传统组合方法只能处理小规模模式或中等规模图，亟需可扩展且灵活的深度学习方法。

Method: 提出GraphDETR框架，将子图检测视为集合预测问题：用图神经网络编码目标图，通过一组固定数量的可学习查询向量和Transformer解码器，在单次前向传播中联合预测所有模式出现位置，使用二分匹配实现端到端训练。

Result: GraphDETR能够检测多种模式（分子结构、环、团、模糊模式），模式规模达50个节点，目标图规模达1000个节点；在ChEMBL分子功能团检测任务中AP100=91.2。

Conclusion: GraphDETR成功将子图检测从传统的精确结构匹配扩展到近似匹配场景，在保持灵活性的同时实现了良好的检测性能，为大规模图上的子图检测提供了新的深度学习范式。

Abstract: Subgraph detection seeks to identify whether and where instances of query patterns occur within a larger graph. This problem is fundamental across scientific domains and is closely related to subgraph isomorphism, which is NP-complete, limiting combinatorial approaches to small patterns or moderately sized graphs. We introduce GraphDETR, a deep learning framework that formulates subgraph detection as a set prediction problem, analogous to DETR in object detection. GraphDETR encodes the target graph with a graph neural network, and employs a fixed set of learnable query vectors, decoded via a transformer decoder, to predict all pattern occurrences jointly in a single forward pass. This is enabled by training the model end-to-end with bipartite matching. Unlike traditional combinatorial methods that only solve exact structural matching, GraphDETR naturally extends to approximate matching, enabling detection beyond exact pattern correspondence. Empirically, we show that GraphDETR can detect diverse patterns, such as molecular structures, cycles, cliques, and fuzzy patterns of up to 50 nodes, in target graphs with up to 1000 nodes. We further evaluate on molecular functional group detection over the ChEMBL dataset, where GraphDETR predicts the complete set of functional groups per molecule, achieving a strong performance of $\text{AP}_{100} = 91.2$.

</details>


### [167] [Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator for HEC-RAS 2D Water-Surface Elevation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06385)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Mahdi Abdelguerfi,Julian Simeonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原生非均匀计算单元的跨数据集评估框架，用于HEC-RAS 2D中求解器一致的水面高程预测。引入LRFIO（无强迫、基于增量的学习型替代模型），通过层级响应结构（持续性→全局校准惯性→分段响应场惯性）实现自适应复杂度控制，在四个基准测试中最大验证遗憾4.30%，部署时间0.003-0.242秒，比HEC-RAS加速约2.75×10^4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型替代模型在HEC-RAS 2D水动力模拟中存在两个问题：1）栅格重映射引入插值误差；2）信息访问混杂导致评估偏差。需要一种直接在原生非均匀计算单元上评估、且能显式分离各类输入信息的评估框架，同时探索是否可以通过增量式学习构建高效且可控复杂度的替代模型。

Method: 1）提出跨数据集评估协议，直接在原始非均匀计算单元上评估，显式分离静态项目输入、当前水力状态、项目输入强迫、校准衍生量和未来求解器输出目标。2）引入LRFIO，这是一种无强迫、基于增量的学习型替代模型，从已求解的HEC-RAS轨迹中校准惯性响应算子，通过闭合形式原生单元滚动部署。3）采用基例优先响应层次：持续性预测→全局校准惯性→分段响应场惯性，将分割、残差校正和神经化惯性作为可学习建模选择，仅当验证证据证明其成本合理时才保留额外复杂度。

Result: 在四个多样化HEC-RAS 2D基准测试中评估，LRFIO对不同域保留不同响应结构，展现自适应学习复杂度。选择器审计显示受控复杂度，最大验证遗憾4.30%。部署时保留的滚动时间范围为0.003秒至0.242秒，Beaver Bayou实测-求解对比估计加速比达2.75×10^4（经时间步归一化）。

Conclusion: 当前原生单元增量是强大的求解器条件化预测支架，附加的响应场、神经或空间复杂度仅应在经验证明确有必要时才保留。LRFIO的自适应复杂度控制策略为大规模水动力模拟的高效替代建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This article presents a cross-dataset evaluation of learned native-cell surrogate models for solver-consistent water-surface elevation (WSE) prediction in HEC-RAS 2D. To avoid raster remapping error and information-access confounding, surrogates are evaluated directly on the original nonuniform computational cells under an explicit policy that separates static project inputs, current hydraulic state, project-input forcing, calibration-derived quantities, and future solver-output targets. We introduce the Learned Response-Field Inertia Operator (LRFIO), a no-forcing, increment-based learned surrogate that calibrates an inertial response operator from solved HEC-RAS trajectories and deploys the retained operator through closed-form native-cell rollout. LRFIO evaluates a base-case-first response hierarchy consisting of persistence, global calibrated inertia, and segmented response-field inertia. Segmentation, residual correction, and neuralized inertia are treated as learnable modeling choices, with added complexity retained only when validation evidence justifies its cost. Evaluated across four diverse HEC-RAS 2D benchmarks, LRFIO retains different response structures for different domains, demonstrating adaptive learned complexity. The selector audit shows controlled complexity with a maximum validation regret of 4.30%. During deployment, retained rollout times range from 0.003 s to 0.242 s, and the Beaver Bayou measured-solve comparison gives an estimated 2.75 x 10^4 horizon-normalized speedup over HEC-RAS. These results indicate that the current native-cell increment is a strong solver-conditioned predictive scaffold and that added response-field, neural, or spatial complexity should be retained only when empirically justified.

</details>


### [168] [Proper Scoring Rules for Right-Censored Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06393)
*Jef Jonkers,Glenn Van Wallendael,Luc Duchateau,Sofie Van Hoecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于右删失生存结果的合理评分框架，通过将预测分布映射到观测数据分布上，构建了适用于固定删失时间和随机删失时间的评分规则，并验证了其性质与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的合理评分规则无法直接应用于右删失数据（事件时间仅部分观测），需要开发适用于删失场景的评分框架。

Method: 先将预测分布通过删失机制映射，再对诱导出的观测数据分布应用原始合理评分，从而得到局部化评分（固定删失时间）和边缘化评分（随机删失时间）。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能在多种删失机制下正确排序oracle预测，而依赖预测的加权评分会出现排序反转；删失engression在删失结果上的训练效果显著优于朴素方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在条件独立删失下保持合理性和严格合理性（在可识别区域），统一了删失似然和IPCW准则，并扩展了CRPS、pinball损失、Brier分数和能量分数等常用评分。

Abstract: Proper scoring rules provide a rigorous theoretical basis for the training and evaluation of probabilistic forecasts. However, in the presence of right censoring, the event time is only partially observed, rendering conventional scoring rules inapplicable in their standard form. We propose a framework for proper scoring of right-censored survival outcomes based on a simple idea: first, map the predictive distribution through the censoring mechanism, then apply the underlying proper score on the induced observed-data law. This yields localized scores for fixed censoring times and marginalized scores when the censoring time is random or only partially observed. The resulting construction recovers familiar right-censored likelihood and IPCW-type criteria within a coherent framework, while also yielding right-censored versions of the CRPS, pinball loss, Brier score, and energy score. We show that the marginalized score is proper under conditional independent censoring and strictly proper on the identifiable region. The same principle also leads to censored engression, a sample-based learning objective for multivariate right-censored survival modeling. In experiments, our scores correctly rank the oracle forecast across several censoring regimes, whereas forecast-dependent plug-in weighted scores can exhibit ranking reversals. Censored engression likewise substantially improves over naive training on censored outcomes.

</details>


### [169] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了关系学习领域中传统扁平化排行榜评估方法存在的系统性偏差，并提出了一种基于曲率分层的评估框架（CurvBench），通过对14个数据集和18个模型的实验，证明模型性能本质上是几何依赖的，而非普遍可迁移的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关系学习评估过度依赖扁平化排行榜，将不同数据集的平均性能视为统一指标，隐含地假设了数据具有均匀的底层结构。作者证明这种假设引入了系统性偏差：掩盖了模型在不同几何特性下的性能差异，并可能导致对模型泛化能力的误导性结论。

Method: 提出了曲率分层评估框架（CurvBench），将数据集按曲率划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个区间。在14个数据集上评估了18个代表性模型，涵盖图卷积网络（GCNs）、图基础模型（GFMs）和表格学习方法。

Result: 模型排名在每个曲率区间内高度稳定，但跨区间时发生显著变化，表明性能本质上是几何依赖的。特别发现GFMs在某些曲率区间相比几何对齐的GNNs存在收益递减现象。

Conclusion: 提出了一种几何感知的评估协议，相比标准聚合基准能够得到更可靠和可解释的比较结果。作者将代码、曲率分层数据集划分和评估工具开源，以支持未来关系学习方法的可重复和严谨评估。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>


### [170] [Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418)
*Thomas T. Zhang,Alok Shah,Yifei Zhang,Vincent Zhang,Nikolai Matni,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为双预条件（DoPr）的优化范式，通过结合梯度预条件和激活预条件，专门针对训练与部署间存在测试时反馈（TTF）失配的深度学习场景（如自回归语言建模、流生成模型、机器人策略学习），有效缓解误差累积，提升下游任务性能，且这种提升并不总是伴随验证损失的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度学习的许多现代应用中，模型训练时使用一步预测损失（如L2回归、交叉熵），但在部署时需要进行自回归式地滚动预测，导致训练/验证损失与下游指标（如任务成功率、生成质量）之间存在显著的失配现象，且该失配随任务长度增长而加剧。尽管已有工作通过数据筛选、架构设计和目标函数设计来缓解这一问题，但优化视角尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出双预条件（DoPr）优化范式，将梯度预条件（如Adam、Muon中使用的）与激活预条件（如KFAC中使用的）相结合。DoPr是一种即插即用的优化干预手段，无需修改模型架构或目标函数。

Result: 在多种TTF设置下（自回归语言建模、流生成模型、机器人策略学习），DoPr相较于基线优化器显著提升了下游测试时性能（如任务成功率、生成质量）。值得注意的是，这些测试时性能的提升并不总是与验证损失的改善同步出现。

Conclusion: DoPr证明了优化作为缓解TTF问题的新设计轴的有效性；同时，验证损失与测试时性能之间的非同步性对传统评估方法提出了新问题，提示需要重新思考如何合理评估使用一步监督目标训练的模型。

Abstract: Many modern applications of deep learning involve training a neural network via a one-step prediction loss (e.g., $L^2$ regression, cross-entropy), but deploy the network by rolling out along its own predictions. Key examples include autoregressive language modeling, flow-based generative modeling, and robot policy learning. It is well-documented that these settings induce a phenomenon we call test-time feedback (TTF): the mismatch between the training/validation loss and downstream metrics of interest, such as task success rate and generation quality, which grows with task length. While data curation, architecture, and objective design have been proposed to combat train-test shift in TTF settings, this paper proposes optimization as a new design axis to mitigate error accumulation. Specifically, we introduce a new optimization paradigm called double-preconditioning (DoPr) uniquely tailored to the challenges of TTF. DoPr combines gradient-wise preconditioning, as in Adam and Muon, with activation-wise preconditioning (AP), such as in KFAC. We show that the addition of AP yields a drop-in intervention for increasing downstream model performance across a range of TTF settings. Interestingly, these gains in test-time performance do not consistently accompany improvements in validation loss, opening new questions about how to properly evaluate models trained with one-step supervised objectives.

</details>


### [171] [Causal Atlases from Entropic Inference: Bayesian Networks beyond Optimal DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06440)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵的推理方法生成因果关系的“图谱集”，通过最大熵系综采样多个等价的有向无环图（DAG），以捕捉数据中固有的因果结构歧义性。在2节点和20节点的线性结构方程模型模拟噪声数据上，证明优化得到的单一DAG可能包含因果伪影，而等价精度的拓扑结构之间并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯网络构建依赖优化方法，但底层数据可能允许多条因果链，优化方法难以捕捉这种多重因果关系的歧义性，需要更忠实于数据多样性的因果表示框架。

Method: 采用基于熵的推理方法，在最大熵系综下采样多个图结构，量化因果关系中固有的结构歧义性；在2节点和20节点线性结构方程模型的模拟噪声数据上进行验证。

Result: 优化得到的DAG可能包含在等价精度拓扑中不一致的因果伪影；通过最大熵系综可以量化因果结构中的歧义性。

Conclusion: 熵基推理能够生成与数据一致的多个因果图谱，为表示数据中固有的因果歧义性提供了更忠实于数据的框架。

Abstract: Data-driven causal relationship identification is pertinent to advancing understanding of complex systems both within and beyond science. Bayesian networks offer a probabilistic method for modelling generic causal relationships via directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). However, typical techniques for constructing Bayesian networks rely on optimization, which can be ill-suited for learning causal relationships because the underlying data may admit multiple chains of causation. More data-faithful representations of causal relationships would provide frameworks for constructing multiple causal maps that are consistent with the variability that is inherent in underlying data. Here, we show that entropy-based inference generates atlases of plausible causal relationships that are consistent with underlying data. On simulated noisy data of 2- and 20-node linear structural equation models, we sample a maximum-entropy ensemble of graphs that allow us to quantify the inherent structural ambiguity in underlying causal relationships. Our method shows that "optimized" DAGs can contain causal artifacts are not consistent across equivalently accurate topologies.

</details>


### [172] [In-Context Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458)
*Alexander Möllers,Marvin Sextro,Julius Hense,Gabriel Dernbach,Klaus-Robert Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Perceiver架构的上下文学习器，通过在合成数据上预训练，实现从少量标注包中解决多实例学习任务，无需梯度更新即可在12个MIL基准上超越需要任务特定训练的监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL算法在低标签场景下表现不佳：灵活模型容易过拟合，而刚性模型无法适应任务需求。因此需要一种能够从少量标注包中高效学习的MIL方法。

Method: 采用Perceiver风格架构的上下文学习器，在合成数据上进行预训练。设计了不同的合成数据生成器以捕捉互补的归纳偏置，并在推理时通过单次前向传播完成分类，无需梯度更新。

Result: 在12个MIL基准测试上，预训练模型在混合生成器上取得最佳平均性能，超越了需要任务特定训练的监督基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于合成数据预训练的上下文学习器能够有效解决低标签场景下的MIL问题，混合多种合成数据生成器可以继承各自优势，实现更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) addresses problems where supervision is available at the level of bags of instances and has been successfully applied in fields ranging from computational pathology to satellite imagery. Nevertheless, existing algorithms struggle in the low-label regime that characterizes many real-world applications. Flexible models overfit and rigid ones fail to adapt to the task at hand. We show that pretraining an in-context learner with a Perceiver-style architecture on synthetic data yields a model that can solve new tasks from a handful of labeled bags. At inference time, classification happens in a single forward pass and requires no gradient updates. We propose and investigate different synthetic data generators for bag-structured data and find that they capture complementary inductive biases. A model pretrained on a mixture of these generators inherits their per-task strengths and achieves the best average performance across twelve MIL benchmarks, outperforming supervised baselines that require task-specific training.

</details>


### [173] [Event Detection for Parameter-to-KPI Dependency Learning for AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06459)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对AI-RAN/O-RAN架构中多AI控制函数相互干扰难以检测的问题，本文提出将连续遥测数据转化为二值事件指示器的显著性检测方法，并引入合成闭环流量生成器进行评估，实验表明该方法能在信号足够强时可靠恢复潜在依赖结构，阈值校准是影响检测质量的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络中多个AI驱动的控制函数同时运行时会产生难以从原始数据中检测到的相互干扰，缺乏一种可靠、可解释的依赖结构来捕获控制参数与网络性能指标之间的因果关系。

Method: 将连续遥测数据转化为二值事件指示器的过程形式化为显著性检测问题；引入合成闭环流量生成器（含植入的潜在依赖关系）作为受控评估环境；构建基于机器学习的依赖恢复流水线。

Result: 当信号与背景变化充分分离时，所提流水线能可靠地从含噪连续轨迹中恢复潜在依赖结构；阈值校准是控制事件检测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该工作为自适应AI-RAN控制系统中可解释的依赖学习奠定了基础性步骤。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks are expected to rely on multiple concurrent AI-driven control functions that optimize different network objectives simultaneously, particularly in AI-integrated and open radio access network architectures such as AI Radio Access Network (AI-RAN) and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN). When these functions interact, they can interfere with one another in ways that are difficult to detect from raw network data alone. A key missing piece for managing such interactions is a reliable, interpretable dependency structure that captures which control parameters are actively influencing which network performance outcomes at any given time. This paper focuses on the event-detection step needed to support such dependency learning by converting noisy continuous telemetry into binary indicators of parameter activity and KPI response. The central difficulty is that not every fluctuation in the data reflects a genuine control interaction, so the method must distinguish real parameter-outcome relationships from background variation. Because real AI-RAN traffic traces with known parameter-KPI ground truth are difficult to obtain, we introduce a synthetic closed-loop traffic generator with planted latent dependencies. We use this controlled telemetry to evaluate a machine-learning-based dependency recovery pipeline that formulates the conversion of continuous traces into binary event indicators as a significance-detection problem. Experimental evaluation shows that the proposed pipeline reliably recovers the latent dependency structure from noisy continuous traces when the signal is sufficiently separated from background variation, while highlighting threshold calibration as the key factor controlling event-detection quality. These results constitute a foundational step toward interpretable dependency learning for adaptive AI-RAN control systems.

</details>


### [174] [PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470)
*Senmiao Wang,Tiantian Fang,Haoran Zhang,Yushun Zhang,Kunxiang Zhao,Alex Schwing,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种预条件层（PC layer），通过多项式预条件器对权重矩阵进行奇异值谱重塑，确保LLM训练过程中权重稳定，训练后无推理开销，在Llama-1B上优于标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练过程中权重矩阵条件数失衡导致训练不稳定，需要一种能保证权重条件数稳定且不增加推理开销的方法。

Method: 设计预条件层（PC layer），通过低次多项式预条件器对权重矩阵的奇异值谱进行重塑；训练后可将预条件权重合并回原架构，不引入推理开销。

Result: 在Llama-1B预训练任务中，PC层在AdamW和Muon优化器下均优于标准Transformer；理论证明均匀约束每层奇异值可保证深度线性网络梯度下降的几何收敛性。

Conclusion: PC层是一种有效的权重参数化方法，可稳定LLM训练，理论上有收敛保证，实际中有性能提升且零推理开销。

Abstract: We propose a preconditioning (PC) layer, a weight parameterization via polynomial preconditioner that ensures stable weight conditioning throughout LLM training. The PC module reshapes the singular-value spectrum of weight matrices via low-degree polynomial preconditioning. After training, the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture, incurring no inference overhead. We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-training, for both the AdamW and Muon optimizers. Theoretically, we justify this spectrum-control principle by proving that uniformly bounding each layer's singular values ensures geometric convergence of gradient descent to global minima, for certain deep linear networks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer.

</details>


### [175] [RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475)
*Mykyta Ielanskyi,Kajetan Schweighofer,Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RREDCoT方法，利用模型自身进行奖励重分配以解决GRPO在CoT训练中的高方差延迟奖励问题，无需额外生成即可近似最优信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等算法在CoT训练中面临延迟奖励问题，蒙特卡洛方法方差高且计算开销大，亟需高效的信用分配方法。

Method: 提出RREDCoT，利用模型自身近似最优奖励重分配，无需额外生成；对比MC采样和多种归因方法，分析CoT分段和状态值估计等关键因素。

Result: RREDCoT相比MC采样和多种归因方法具有优势，在延迟奖励场景下实现了更高效的信用分配。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身进行奖励重分配是解决CoT训练中延迟奖励问题的有效途径，在计算效率和分配质量上优于传统MC方法。

Abstract: Recent advancements in reasoning language models have been driven by Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning. Most often, these rely on the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm or modifications thereof to steer the models to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces. The final answer can only be verified, and the reward assigned, after the CoT trace is complete, making it a delayed reward problem. GRPO and its modifications correspond to Monte Carlo methods in standard RL, which are known to suffer from high variance. A possible solution to this problem is the redistribution of rewards through credit assignment, where segments of the CoT trace that are important for arriving at the desirable solution are emphasized by assigning a higher reward. While Monte Carlo sampling can be used to provide an unbiased estimate of intermediate state values, its computational overhead makes it unsuitable for train-time credit assignment in long contexts at high granularity. We introduce RREDCoT (Reward REDistribution for Chain of Thoughts), which utilizes the model itself to approximate the optimal reward redistribution without additional generation. We investigate the advantages of our method compared to MC sampling and several attribution methods. We further analyze several aspects relevant to the construction of the redistribution such as segmentation of CoT traces and state value estimation.

</details>


### [176] [Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479)
*Akarsh Kumar,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督记忆训练(SMT)方法，通过将RNN训练转化为一步记忆转换标签的监督学习，实现时间并行训练，消除循环梯度传播问题，在语言建模和像素序列建模任务上优于BPTT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BPTT存在时间顺序限制并行性、梯度消失/爆炸导致难以学习长程依赖的问题，需要一种能够并行训练且稳定捕获长程依赖的RNN训练方法。

Method: SMT: (1) 使用基于Transformer的编码器训练预测状态目标，提取记忆标签(m_t, x_{t+1}) → m_{t+1}；(2) 将RNN训练简化为对一步记忆转换标签的监督学习；(3) 解耦"记住什么"与"如何更新记忆"，实现时间并行训练和O(1)长度的稳定梯度路径。

Result: SMT在语言建模和像素序列建模等任务上，预训练多种RNN架构时均优于BPTT。

Conclusion: SMT使非线性RNN能更好地捕获长程依赖并实现并行训练，有望解锁构建时间抽象的大规模模型。

Abstract: Training recurrent neural networks (RNNs) requires assigning credit across long sequences of computations. Standard backpropagation through time (BPTT) addresses this problem poorly: it is sequential in time, limiting parallelism, and suffers from vanishing or exploding gradients, making long-range associations difficult to learn. We propose Supervised Memory Training (SMT), a method for training nonlinear RNNs that sidesteps recurrent credit propagation entirely by reducing RNN training to supervised learning on one-step memory transition labels $(m_t, x_{t+1}) \rightarrow m_{t+1}$. SMT acquires these memory labels by training a Transformer-based encoder on a predictive state objective--retaining only information from the past necessary to predict the future. By decoupling what to remember from how to update memory, SMT enables time-parallel RNN training with a stable $O(1)$ length gradient path between any two tokens--without ever unrolling the RNN. We find that SMT outperforms BPTT when pretraining various RNN architectures on tasks like language modeling and pixel sequence modeling. SMT enables nonlinear RNNs to better capture long-range dependencies and train in parallel, potentially unlocking the scaling of models that build temporal abstractions of past experience.

</details>


### [177] [Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486)
*Mingyang Liu,Asuman Ozdaglar,Tiancheng Yu,Kaiqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的博弈论遗憾度量——重复策略遗憾（RP-Regret），用于评估自适应对手环境下的重复博弈，并设计了三种算法实现次线性遗憾，证明其能引导学习子博弈完美均衡并促进合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的外部遗憾（external regret）无法衡量自适应对手环境中的反事实推理能力，需要一种更能反映重复博弈特性的遗憾度量，以支持更强的比较器和更少的对手约束，并能在所有玩家最小化该遗憾时找到更好的均衡。

Method: 定义了RP-Regret，识别了实现次线性遗憾的必要条件（策略变化程度、比较器和对手的记忆约束），提出了三种算法：基于优化oracle的非凸优化、凸线性化代理最小化、以及直接最小化（对手策略缓慢变化时）。

Result: 实现了RP-Regret的次线性上界；当所有玩家运行算法时，可学习到重复博弈的某些子博弈完美均衡；实验表明该方法在Stag-Hunt等博弈中能引导出更合作的、效用更高的解。

Conclusion: RP-Regret是比现有遗憾度量更适合自适应对手重复博弈的指标，通过适当的算法设计可以在非凸策略空间中实现次线性遗憾，并促进均衡学习和合作行为。

Abstract: In this paper, we study regret minimization in repeated games with \emph{adaptive} opponents who can respond based on histories of play. The standard metric of \emph{external regret} in online learning is known to fail to capture such adaptivity. To account for players' counterfactual reasoning, we introduce {\tt Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)}, a game-theoretic metric that measures the difference between the \emph{realized} and the \emph{best-in-hindsight} accumulated utility when all players can \emph{respond} to the history of play. Compared to existing regret notions in this setting, ours is native to repeated game playing, enabling stronger comparators and opponents with fewer constraints, while maintaining the possibility of finding better equilibria when all players minimize it. We first identify necessary conditions for obtaining {\tt RP-Regret} sublinear in time, on the variation of the player's comparator strategies in the regret definition and on the memories of both the comparator and opponents' strategies. We then study additional conditions and provable algorithms to minimize {\tt RP-Regret}, which is by definition \emph{non-convex} in the strategy space. To address this challenge, we propose three algorithms: (i) one based on an optimization oracle, as assumed in some prior work in online non-convex learning; (ii) one that minimizes a convex and \emph{linearized} surrogate of {\tt RP-Regret} at each iteration; (iii) one that directly minimizes {\tt RP-Regret} when opponents change strategies slowly. Furthermore, when all players can run algorithms to minimize the {\tt RP-Regret} (or its linearized variant), certain subgame perfect equilibria of the repeated game can be learned. We also provide experiments showing that minimizing our regret notions can lead to more cooperative solutions with higher utility in games such as Stag-Hunt.

</details>


### [178] [TailLoR: Protecting Principal Components in Parameter-Efficient Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06494)
*Marius Dragoi,Ioana Pintilie,Alexandra Dragomir,Antonio Barbalau,Florin Brad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailLoR 提出了一种基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法，利用预训练权重的奇异基作为固定参考框架，仅更新奇异值矩阵，并通过软谱惩罚抑制对主奇异方向的更新，从而将细粒度适应导向长尾谱坐标，减少连续学习中的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于谱分解的参数高效微调方法在持续学习中取得了进展，但如何在不干扰已学知识的同时实现高效适应仍是一个挑战。TailLoR 旨在通过将更新限制在长尾谱方向上来解决干扰问题。

Method: 利用预训练权重的奇异基 U 和 V 作为固定参考框架，仅学习对奇异值矩阵的低秩更新；引入软谱惩罚项，抑制与主导奇异方向对齐的更新，从而将细粒度适应导向灵活的长尾谱坐标。

Result: 通过软谱惩罚减少了对主奇异方向的干扰，提升了持续学习场景下的适应能力和知识保持能力。

Conclusion: TailLoR 通过将参数更新约束在长尾谱方向上，有效减少了持续学习中的灾难性干扰，为基于谱分解的持续学习方法提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning methods based on spectral decomposition have enabled progress in Continual Learning. In this paper we introduce TailLoR, which utilizes the singular bases U and V of the pre-trained weights as a fixed reference frame to learn a low-rank update applied to the singular value matrix. A soft spectral penalty discourages updates aligned with dominant singular directions, reducing interference while routing fine-grained adaptation into the highly flexible, long-tail spectral coordinates.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Quantum Circuit Complexity as a Measure of Particle Creation in Bouncing Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05235)
*Samak Boonpan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量方法，对非奇异反弹宇宙背景下的量子标量场电路复杂度演化进行了精确非微扰分析，发现复杂度在反弹点保持有限且由量子态压缩主导，反弹后复杂度增长与宇宙学粒子产生强相关，其中"啁啾"贡献作为几何转变的存储器，量化了非平凡时空几何中粒子产生的信息成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越以往微扰方法的局限，建立精确的非微扰框架来研究反弹宇宙背景中量子场的电路复杂度演化，从而更准确地理解复杂度与时空几何之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量方法代替传统的微扰方法，对反弹宇宙背景下的量子标量场进行非微扰分析，精确推导复杂度在反弹过程中的演化行为。

Result: 复杂度在反弹点保持有限，主要由时空收缩导致的量子态压缩主导；反弹后复杂度增长与宇宙学粒子产生存在强相关性；"啁啾"贡献作为几何转变的存储器，量化了粒子产生的信息成本。

Conclusion: 电路复杂度中的"啁啾"贡献充当了时空几何转变的几何存储器，有效量化了非平凡时空几何中粒子产生所对应的信息成本，揭示了复杂度与粒子产生之间的深层关联。

Abstract: We investigate the evolution of circuit complexity for a quantum scalar field in a non-singular bouncing cosmological background. Unlike previous perturbative approaches, we utilize the Lewis-Riesenfeld invariant method to derive an exact non-perturbative framework for the complexity evolution across the bounce. We demonstrate that the complexity remains finite at the bounce, dominated by the squeezing of the quantum state due to spacetime contraction. Crucially, we find that the post-bounce growth of complexity exhibits a strong correlation with cosmological particle production. Our analysis reveals that the "chirping" contribution to complexity acts as a geometric memory of the transition, effectively quantifying the information cost of particle creation in non-trivial spacetime geometries.

</details>


### [180] [Equation of State Parameters for Fluid of Stringy Extended Objects in Cosmology with Cosmological Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05243)
*Soon-Tae Hong,Bum-Hoon Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在高维宇宙学（HDC）中构建了弦论扩展物体（既有质量也有无质量）的强能量条件（SECs），推导出态方程参数w≥-(D-4)/D（D≥5），并揭示了该约束在辐射和物质主导时期均成立。同时探讨了HDC中EoS参数与四维Hawking-Penrose极限下点粒子EoS参数之间的关系，并分别研究了弱能量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维宇宙学背景下，研究弦论扩展物体（而非传统点粒子）的能量条件，以扩展对早期宇宙和额外维度物理的理解。

Method: 通过构建质量和无质量弦论扩展物体的强能量条件（SECs），推导出态方程参数的下界约束，并与Hawking-Penrose极限下的点粒子结果进行比较；同时分析弱能量条件。

Result: 得到EoS参数w≥-(D-4)/D（D≥5）的通用约束，该约束在辐射和物质主导时期均有效；阐明了HDC中EoS参数与四维点粒子EoS参数之间的关系；将EoS参数分解为点粒子属性、宇宙学常数和扩展物体自由度三部分贡献。

Conclusion: 弦论SECs对w施加了一个通用约束，揭示了高维弦论扩展物体与四维点粒子在能量条件上的联系。

Abstract: We construct the strong energy conditions (SECs) for both massive and massless stringy extended objects in the higher dimensional cosmology (HDC) with cosmological constant $Λ$. Exploiting these conditions, we find the equation of state (EoS) parameters \mbox{$w\geq -(D-4)/D$} for both the massive and massless stringy extended objects in $D$ {$(D\geq 5)$} dimensional cosmology. The stringy SECs impose a universal constraint on $w$ that remains valid across both radiation- and matter-dominated eras. We elucidate the relations between the EoS parameter in the HDC with cosmological constant and that of Hawking--Penrose limit for the massive and massless point particles in the four dimensions. We evaluate the EoS parameters in terms of the contributions from the point particle property, cosmological constant, and extended object degrees of freedom, respectively. We also investigate the weak energy condition for the massive and massless stringy extended objects in the HDC, and those for the massive and massless point particles in the four dimensions,~respectively.

</details>


### [181] [Running Vacuum in the expanding Universe: a unified QFT paradigm for Inflation and Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05352)
*Joan Solà Peracaula*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出运行真空模型（RVM），基于量子场论在弯曲时空中的量子效应，使真空能量密度成为哈勃速率及其时间导数的函数，从而将暗能量和暴胀统一在一个理论框架内，无需引入暴胀子场，且与DESI观测数据中暗能量动态演化的倾向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ΛCDM模型中刚性Λ项难以解释暗能量本质，且与观测存在长期矛盾。宇宙膨胀背景下，随时间演化的Λ更合理，因此需要构建一个基于量子场论的动态真空能量框架。

Method: 在弯曲时空量子场论框架下，RVM将真空能量密度ρ_vac表示为H及其时间导数的函数（含H^2项和早期H^4项），牛顿常数G随ln H对数演化。早期高能标下H^4项驱动暴胀（RVM暴胀），无需暴胀子场。

Result: RVM中ρ_vac随宇宙膨胀缓慢演化（δρ_vac~O(m_Pl²H²)），为暗能量提供动力学起源；早期H^4项驱动快速暴胀；G呈对数演化。该框架与DESI测量结果中动态暗能量优于刚性Λ项的结论一致。

Conclusion: RVM将暗能量和暴胀统一于量子场论框架，动态真空能量密度具有理论自洽性和观测支持，为解释宇宙整体演化提供了更根本的理论基础。

Abstract: The concordance $Λ$CDM model, based on a rigid $Λ$-term for the entire cosmic history, has been in crisis for a long time. In our expanding Universe, an evolving $Λ$ with the expansion is intuitively much more reasonable. In the running vacuum model (RVM) framework, based on quantum field theory (QFT) in curved spacetime, quantum effects induce a vacuum energy density (VED) $ρ_{\rm vac}=Λ/(8πG)$ which is a function of the Hubble rate $H$ and its time derivatives, $ρ_{\rm vac}=ρ_{\rm vac}(H, \dot{H},\ddot{H},\dots)$. Currently, $ρ_{\rm vac}$ evolves very slowly with the expansion, $δρ_{\rm vac}\sim {\cal O}(m_{Pl} ^2 H^2)$, and this fact provides a possible fundamental origin of dark energy (DE), conceived as dynamical vacuum energy. In the RVM, Newton's $G$ is also evolving, but much more slowly (logarithmically with $H$): $G=G(\ln H)$. In the very early universe, the vacuum fluctuations induce higher (even) powers, e.g. $\sim H^4$, capable of triggering fast inflation in a very short period, in which $H$ is very large and approximately constant. This is the mechanism of `RVM-inflation'. It does not require an `inflaton' field since inflation is brought about by pure QFT effects on the dynamical background. It differs from Starobinsky's inflation, where $H$ is never constant. Furthermore, the dynamics of $ρ_{\rm vac}(H)$ and $G(H)$ can also have implications on the frequently discussed possibility that the fundamental `constants' of Nature can be mildly evolving with the cosmic expansion. Putting things together, a unified QFT framework of dark energy and inflation ensues as a realistic theory for the description of the universe as a whole on fundamental grounds. In it, dynamical VED is predicted and is much welcomed, since it fits in with current DESI measurements, preferring dynamical DE over a rigid $Λ$ term.

</details>


### [182] [Spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat Berwald vacuum solutions in Finsler gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05427)
*Nicoleta Voicu,Diana - Maria Birla,Christian Pfeifer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了球对称Berwald-Finsler时空中的引力真空方程，发现并完全求解了唯一一类满足渐近平坦性和良好因果结构的非Ricci平坦解，得到了三个非平凡的精确真空解族，这些(α,β)-Finsler解为超越黎曼几何描述、建模致密天体外部引力场提供了新候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找球对称Finsler时空中非Ricci平坦的渐近平坦真空解，以更准确地描述致密天体（如黑洞、中子星）外部的引力场，超越传统伪黎曼几何的局限性。

Method: 在SO(3)对称性假设下，求解Berwald-Finsler时空的Finsler引力真空方程，施加渐近平坦性和因果结构约束，筛选可行解类并完全求解。

Result: 发现仅有一类球对称Berwald时空同时满足渐近平坦性和良好因果结构；完全求解了该类的真空方程，得到三个非Ricci平坦的精确解族，这些解属于(α,β)-Finsler类型，由伪黎曼度规和1-形式构造。

Conclusion: 证明Finsler几何中存在SO(3)对称、渐近平坦的非Ricci平坦真空解，为致密天体引力场的非黎曼描述提供了具体候选解。

Abstract: So-called Berwald-Finsler spacetimes are Finsler spacetimes that are closest to pseudo-Riemannian geometry, as their canonical nonlinear connection defines an affine connection on spacetime. In spherical symmetry, these geometries can be used to describe the gravitational field outside of compact objects. We solve the Finsler gravity vacuum equation for $SO(3)$-symmetric Berwald spacetimes that are asyptotically flat, but not Ricci flat. We find that among all spherically symmetric Berwald spacetimes, only one class is compatible with asymptotic flatness and a well defined causal structure. For this class, we completely solve the Finsler gravity vacuum equation and find three families of non-Ricci flat solutions -- which represent the first non-trivial, exact spherically symmetric vacuum solutions. They are so-called $(α,β)$-Finsler spacetimes that are constructed from a pseudo-Riemannnian metric and a 1-form. In particular, we show, by providing a concrete example, that in Finsler geometry there exist $SO(3)$-symmetric, asymptotically flat vacuum solutions that are not Ricci flat; these solutions are promising candidates to model the gravitational field around compact objects, beyond their Riemannian description.

</details>


### [183] [Curvature conditions for generalized singularity theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05453)
*Jeremías Daín,Gustavo Dotti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高余维紧致 trapped 子流形作为时空奇点预测因子的适用性，发现特定曲率条件并非普遍适用，但某些特殊 CTM 仍可预测奇点，不过无法排除它们与外通信域相交的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将彭罗斯奇点定理从二维 trapped 面推广到更高余维的紧致 trapped 子流形（CTM），以检验其是否也能作为时空奇点的预测因子。

Method: 分析 Class. Quant. Grav. 27, 152002 中提出的曲率条件，考察这些条件对高余维 trapped 子流形焦点预测的适用性。

Result: 发现这些曲率条件在一般情况下不适用，仅对特定类型的 CTM 成立；高余维 CTM 仍可能预测奇点，但无法用标准论证排除它们与外通信域相交。

Conclusion: 高余维 CTM 作为奇点预测因子存在局限性，其有效性依赖于具体曲率条件，且与外通信域的关系问题仍未解决。

Abstract: We study the curvature conditions introduced in [Class. Quant. Grav. 27, 152002] to predict focal points for trapped spacelike submanifolds in spacetimes of arbitrary dimensions, with the purpose of generalizing Penrose's singularity theorem to compact trapped submanifolds (CTMs) of codimension higher than two. We find that these conditions do not apply in general but may apply for specific CTMs. As a result, higher codimension CTMs may still work as singularity predictors, although the possibility that they intersect the domain of outer communications cannot be ruled out using standard arguments.

</details>


### [184] [Improved unimodular black holes with self-consistent renormalization scale identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05507)
*Christopher D. Carone*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过重正化群改进方法，将重正化尺度识别为依赖于改进度量的径向坐标函数，基于最小性和量纲分析得到若干非奇异的幺模黑洞度量，确定了黑洞质量间隙，并讨论了与文献中其他非奇异黑洞度量的定性相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对幺模黑洞度量进行重正化群改进，通过自洽地确定重正化尺度与径向坐标的函数关系，以消除黑洞奇点并获得非奇异的黑洞解。

Method: 采用重正化群改进方法，将重正化尺度识别为依赖于改进度量参数的径向坐标函数，基于最小性和量纲分析选择识别方案，从而得到若干幺模黑洞度量。

Result: 得到了若干非奇异的幺模黑洞度量，确定了黑洞质量间隙，并与文献中其他非奇异黑洞度量存在定性相似性。

Conclusion: 通过自洽的重正化尺度识别，可以消除幺模黑洞的奇点，得到的非奇异黑洞度量具有质量间隙，且与已知的非奇异黑洞解定性相似。

Abstract: We consider the renormalization group improvement of unimodular black hole metrics with an identification of the renormalization scale as a function of the radial coordinate that is self-consistent in that it depends on parameters of the improved metric. Considering identifications that are motivated by minimality and dimensional analysis, we arrive at a number of non-singular unimodular black hole metrics. We determine the black hole mass gaps and note the qualitative similarities to other non-singular black hole metrics that have been discussed in the literature.

</details>


### [185] [Black Bounce Solutions from a Self-Interacting 3-Form Field in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05508)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo,Manuel E. Rodrigues*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一类由自相互作用3-形式场与标量场在广义相对论框架下耦合产生的新型黑弹跳解。通过利用4维时空中的3-形式与1-形式的霍奇对偶性，直接从完整作用量原理出发求解场方程，得到了两类精确解：第一类具有全局幻影标量场和反正切依赖的度规函数，在3-形式耦合消失时退化为Schwarzschild-(anti) de Sitter时空；第二类具有恒定3-形式拉格朗日量和部分正则标量场（仅在弹跳附近为幻影，事件视界外为正则）。两类解均为全局正则且具有非对称视界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造由物质场自然支撑的黑弹跳几何，避免人为预设度规形式的反推重构法，探索3-形式场在广义相对论中作为黑洞-可穿越虫洞平滑插值解物质源的可能性。

Method: 从完整作用量原理出发，考虑3-形式场与标量场耦合的Einstein重力，利用4维中3-形式与1-形式的霍奇对偶性简化场方程，通过直接积分耦合的运动方程得到精确解。采用代数组合与操作处理Einstein方程、标量场方程和3-形式场方程，而非通常的反推重构方法。

Result: 发现两类解：第一类具有全局幻影标量场，度规函数含arctan依赖，耦合消失时退化为Schwarzschild-(A)dS时空；第二类具有恒定3-形式拉格朗日量，标量场在弹跳附近为幻影、视界外为正则（部分正则标量场）。Kretschmann标量有限性验证了两类解的全局正则性，且均表现出3-形式能量密度分布导致的非对称视界结构。

Conclusion: 3-形式黑弹跳框架在数学上自洽且在观测上可行，展示了在纯广义相对论中实现部分正则标量场的新途径，为黑洞-虫洞统一描述提供了新的物质场实现机制。

Abstract: We construct a new class of black-bounce solutions sourced by a self-interacting 3-form field minimally coupled to general relativity and a scalar field. The 3-form field, which naturally arises in string theory, supergravity, and cosmological models, provides the anisotropic effective stresses required to sustain regular geometries that interpolate smoothly between black holes and traversable wormholes. By exploiting the Hodge duality between a 3-form and a 1-form in four dimensions, we reduce the field equations and obtain exact solutions through the direct integration of the coupled equations of motion. In particular, the solutions are derived from algebraic combinations and manipulations of the Einstein, scalar, and 3-form field equations, starting from a complete action principle, without employing the usual reconstruction procedure in which the metric ansatz is imposed a priori and the matter sector is reconstructed afterwards. This approach reveals two distinct classes of solutions. The first one yields a globally phantom scalar field and a metric function with a characteristic arctangent dependence, reducing to the Schwarzschild-(anti) de Sitter spacetime in the limit of vanishing 3-form coupling. The second class produces a constant 3-form Lagrangian and, remarkably, a partially canonical scalar field, namely phantom only near the bounce and canonical outside the event horizon, a feature previously attainable mainly in modified theories of gravity, but which emerges here within pure general relativity. Both families are globally regular, as confirmed by the finiteness of the Kretschmann scalar, and exhibit an asymmetric horizon structure inherited from the 3-form energy-density distribution. These results demonstrate that the 3-form black-bounce framework is both mathematically consistent and observationally viable.

</details>


### [186] [Scalarized extremal black holes in the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory with two U(1) fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05766)
*Xiao Yan Chew,Yun Soo Myung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中标量化的极端黑洞，该理论受N=4超引力玻色子部分启发。发现了两个具有常数次级标量毛的标量化极端黑洞，并通过标准标量化和熵函数方法验证了其精确性。研究暗示了寻找具有初级标量毛的极端黑洞可能并不容易。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受N=4超引力理论启发，探索具有两个U(1)场耦合的Einstein-Maxwell-scalar理论中标量化极端黑洞的存在性和性质，特别关注标量毛的类型（初级vs次级）。

Method: 采用标准标量化和熵函数两种方法，对具有两个不同标量耦合到两个U(1)场的Einstein-Maxwell-scalar理论进行分析，求解并验证标量化极端黑洞解。

Result: 发现了两个具有常数次级标量毛的标量化极端黑洞解，并通过两种独立方法（标准标量化和熵函数方法）精确验证了这些解的一致性。

Conclusion: 在研究的理论框架下，标量化极端黑洞仅支持次级（常数）标量毛，而难以获得具有初级标量毛的极端黑洞解，这为标量化黑洞的毛结构分类提供了重要约束。

Abstract: We study scalarized extremal black holes in the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory with two different scalar couplings to two U(1) fields. This theory is inspired by the bosonic sector of $N=4$ supergravity. Two scalarzied extremal black holes are found with constant secondary scalar hair. We confirm that these are exactly obtained from the standard scalarization and entropy function approach. This may imply that it is not easy to find extremal black holes with primary scalar hair.

</details>


### [187] [Asymptotically-flat Black holes in Bumblebee gravity: Exact solutions and Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05801)
*Jinbo Yang,Zhan-Feng Mai,Dicong Liang,Lijing Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了静态球对称时空中蜂鸟引力理论的解析解，其中蜂鸟矢量场仅含非零时间分量。研究识别了允许渐近平坦黑洞解的参数空间，并分析了这些黑洞的热力学性质，获得了确保斯马尔关系与黑洞热力学第一定律的Y电荷与X势的解析公式。通过新解析结果，验证了早期数值发现并揭示了被遗漏的多个情形，包括：非最小耦合参数ξ>2κ时的无界荷质比、ξ<0过荷解中的可穿越虫洞结构、霍金温度随荷质比变化的非单调转折行为，以及恒Y热容量的两个发散点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在蜂鸟引力理论中，此前主要通过数值方法研究黑洞解及其热力学性质，缺乏完整的解析框架来验证和拓展数值发现。本文旨在构建解析解，以更全面地探索参数空间，并揭示数值分析遗漏的新物理现象。

Method: 采用静态球对称度规假设，令蜂鸟矢量场仅保留时间分量，通过求解场方程获得解析解。在此基础上推导热力学量，利用Y电荷与X势概念确保斯马尔关系和热力学第一定律成立，并通过解析分析系统扫描参数空间。

Result: (i) 当ξ>2κ时，荷质比可无界增长；(ii) ξ<0的过荷解中存在可穿越虫洞构型；(iii) 霍金温度随荷质比变化呈现非单调转折；(iv) 恒Y热容量出现两个发散点。这些结果验证并补充了前人数值工作。

Conclusion: 解析解揭示了蜂鸟引力理论中更丰富的黑洞解结构，包括无界荷质比、虫洞构型及热力学量的奇异行为，为理解该修正引力理论提供了新的理论洞见。
